// [0, 21]. Black on white is 21 and a colour against itself is 1. Invalid => [0, 21]。黑底白字为 21，颜色与自身为 1。无效

// minInkContrast is the WCAG AA floor for large/bold text (3:1). Badge labels => minInkContrast 是 WCAG AA 对大号/粗体文字的下限（3:1）。徽章标签
// needs a hex ink/bg pair on Palette populated only for non-built-in themes; => 在 Palette 上仅对非内置主题填充十六进制的墨色/背景对；
// never change what they were going to draw. => 永远不会改变它们原本要绘制的内容。
// parseHexColor decodes #rgb, #rgba, #rrggbb and #rrggbbaa into 8-bit => parseHexColor 把 #rgb、#rgba、#rrggbb 和 #rrggbbaa 解码为 8 位

// Diff row roles. => Diff 的行角色。
// DiffKind is the role of one row in a line diff. => DiffKind 是行差异中某一行的角色。
// DiffLine is one row of a line diff. Text never carries the +/- marker; the => DiffLine 是行差异的一行。Text 从不携带 +/- 标记；
// DiffLines returns a context/add/delete line diff of two texts. It trims the => DiffLines 返回两段文本的上下文/新增/删除行差异。它先修剪
// both stages use only the standard library. => 两个阶段都只使用标准库。
// diffMiddle aligns the changed region. Over the cap it degrades to del+add => diffMiddle 对齐变化区域。超过上限时它降级为 del+add，
// diffSplit drops the empty element a trailing newline would otherwise add. => diffSplit 丢弃尾随换行本会添加的空元素。
// emitted as a wholesale delete+add, which is still correct, just less tight. => 整体作为删除+新增输出，这仍然正确，只是不够紧凑。
// identical prefix and suffix first, then runs a small LCS over the middle; => 相同的的前缀和后缀，然后对中间部分运行一个小型 LCS；
// maxDiffLCSCells bounds the dynamic-programming table. Beyond this the hunk is => maxDiffLCSCells 限制动态规划表。超过此值时，块被
// rather than allocating an unbounded table. => 而不是分配无界的表。
// renderer adds it so the same helper can be reused by plain-text output. => 渲染器添加它，使同一辅助函数可复用于纯文本输出。

	// by Push or by the final Flush. This guards against silently eating text. => Push 输出，要么由最后的 Flush 输出。这防止悄悄吞掉文本。

	// eight core ones: yellow notices/running tools and inline code. => 八个核心角色之外的两个角色：黄色通知/运行中工具和行内代码。
